## Mandate

Give v5 v4's Inventory STORAGE, MODEL and chat-spine CHOKEPOINTS, whole, at
`322a5488e`'s shape, so that eight sibling lanes compile against ONE
`inventory` module, ONE repository for the carried set, ONE error type with
all thirteen codes, ONE pool, ONE equip chokepoint and ONE fixture plant. The
objects are FILES (`Inventory/**/*.json`, one per object, serialised in
`INVENTORY_FILE_KEYS` order with `JSON.stringify(out, null, 2) + '\n'`),
projected whole through `project_array_into_vault_folder` with a NEW `.json`
ext; the folder machinery the wardrobe owned is LIFTED into
`inventory::item_folders` + `db::item_folder_ops` with every wardrobe string
byte-identical (the wardrobe families stay green through the lift). A v5 boot
ADDS `chats."equippedInventory"` and `characters."canManageInventory"` on a
pre-round instance through two column-gated, UNSTAMPED ensures in v4's
registration order (the P4.D249 / P4.D304 two-shape class — `TEXT` /
`INTEGER` rendered on a fresh instance, `TEXT DEFAULT NULL` / `INTEGER DEFAULT
NULL` appended on an existing one), a NO-OP on an instance v4 already
migrated; a fresh v5 instance provisions the FOUR moves (13–16) together from
the ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump taken at the pin (R-A), so `provisioning_
equivalence` (1d / 1b / 1c) and `migration_index_backfill` — RED on unported
`main` from the pin by design — go green on this branch. `equip_items` /
`unequip_items` / `pick_up_items` plan-then-write inside ONE writer closure
(kit expansion, archetype members INSTANTIATED as vault files, the per-chat
kin cap checked over the WHOLE plan before any write, the roster shelf gate
for character gestures) and RETURN the `InventoryChange` to the caller — they
never notify, never post, never refresh an avatar (P4.D328's
`notify_inventory_changed` does that after the commit). Arrival defaults at
chat create / seat add / merge write `{"<charId>":[]}` EVEN WITH NO INVENTORY
(the stamp every re-seat keys on); `setEquippedInventory`'s two caught-failure
lines are captured pins (R-K). `## Carried and equipped` is a TRAILING section
built on EVERY turn (bug 253 — ungated), AFTER progressions and BEFORE the
inform block. `post_pascal_inventory_change` writes Pascal's line
(`systemKind 'inventory-change'`, NO `pascalMeta`). `RealtimeTopic::Inventory`
is the eighth topic. `characters_read` carries `canManageInventory`;
`chats_read` carries `equippedInventory` — both APPENDED at the END of their
positional marshals, never at DDL position. You do NOT write
`inventory/{refs,context}.rs`, `services/inventory_change_effects.rs`, the
turn containers, any tool, any `Request` variant or route, the image-phrase
pipelines (`appearance_resolution.rs`, `avatar_prompt.rs`, `story_background_
job.rs`, `tools/generate_image.rs`, `character_avatar_job.rs`, `inventory/
image_description.rs` — P4.D326), the `refresh_avatar_for_appearance` rename
(P4.D326), any carrier (`uuid_remap.rs`, the restore orchestrator, `character_
keyed_fields.rs`, the import reconcile, `schema-key-order.json`, the export
schema — P4.D327), `pascal/**` (P4.D329 / P4.D333 / P4.D332 / P4.D331), the
lint (`title_lint*.rs`, `lint_scope.rs`, `tool_gate_keys.rs`, the lint calls,
the `linted` argument, `database_store.rs`'s hooks — P4.D340), `host.rs`'s
geometry block / `terminal_sessions_geometry_repair.rs` (P4.D337),
`db/table_shape.rs` (P4.D331), the SPA, `help/` or `docs/v4/` (P4.D339 — a
`HANDOFF:`). You land C1 under C1's NAMES; a departure from a name is a

## Rulings made at planning (the planner's calls; the human may overrule before lane start — a STOP item if they do)

Round rulings that govern this lane, by reference: **THE PIN** (§R.1 /
§R.2); **R-A** (ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump at the pin, owned HERE,
carrying ALL FOUR moves 13–16 — never hand-pruned); **R-B** (`log_entries`
provisions through the structural pass — your re-dump carries its row, you
add NO ensure, NO stamp, NOTHING to `host.rs` / `restore_vintage_state` /
`zero_migrations_rerun` for it; P4.D331 adds the `STRUCTURAL_TABLES` row);
**R-K** (the two caught-failure lines are yours as captured pins); **R-M**
(the transfers module ports the PIN's shape; the REST edges are P4.D325's);
**R-N** (bug 253 is yours; P4.D322's R-N is RETIRED — the `:2924` doc comment
now reads v4's whole sentence WITH the word "inventory"); **R-O**
(`AggregateScope` / `AggregateObject` are P4.D328's `refs.rs` — not yours);
**R-S** is NOT yours (P4.D327 measures the import's keyed arm); **R-T** (the
transfers error shapes: you port the pin's bytes; the ROUTE-level red is
P4.D328's); **R-G** (no struct-literal reflow anywhere; you add no
`RunCustomToolContext` field). **Version bumps** (§R.6): `quilltap-core` +1
per commit touching it, `quilltap-host` +1 (the two ensure calls in
`host.rs`); `quilltap-harness` NEVER; `quilltap-web`, `quilltap-cli`,
`quilltap-tauri`, the SPA untouched by this lane.

- **R-1 — the four change DATA types are the keystone's, HOMED in `inventory::
  equip`, and P4.D328 RE-EXPORTS them (C1 as amended by the planner on
  2026-10-21 from the P4.D328 drafter's finding).** C1 says `equip_items(…, ctx:
  &InventoryChangeContext, …) -> Result<(EquipResult, Option<InventoryChange>),
  …>` and C2 says `services::inventory_change_effects::{InventoryChangeProvenance,
  InventoryChangeContext, InventoryChange, PendingInventoryChange, …}` are
  P4.D328's. Both hold only if the TYPES exist at `INVENTORY` (P4.D328
  branches from it) under C2's PATH afterwards: so this lane declares the
  four as pure data in `crates/quilltap-core/src/inventory/equip.rs` (v4
  `equip-effects.ts:38-56` + `turn-containers.ts:18-30`; no behaviour — no
  `describe_inventory_change`, no `record_pending…`, no notify) and P4.D328's
  NEW `services/inventory_change_effects.rs` opens with `pub use
  crate::inventory::equip::{InventoryChangeProvenance, InventoryChangeContext,
  InventoryChange, PendingInventoryChange};`. WHY: the alternative — a
  keystone-local stand-in P4.D328 later deletes — rewrites `equip.rs`'s
  signature on a dependent's branch, which §R.10 (o) forbids. The
  `pending` field is `Option<&'a mut HashMap<String, PendingInventoryChange>>`
  (v4's `Map`, keyed `chat_id:character_id` — `${chatId}:${characterId}`; the
  entries are P4.D328's to write) and is NEVER read by any keystone fn (the keystone returns the change; the
  caller nets it). Record the re-export obligation as a `HANDOFF:` line for
  P4.D328 in the lane record. _(The seam the round block left implicit; settled
  by the 2026-10-21 amendment.)_
- **R-2 — `runSerialized` → the writer task; the SYNC in-write forms are the
  primary API, the async forms are ONE `Db::write` each (survey §8 Q3 —
  ruled YES).** v4 serialises per folder (`Inventory:<mp>`) and per chat
  (`inventory-equip:<chatId>`) through one promise-chain map; v5's writer
  task serialises every write, and the READ-plan-WRITE span of
  `equipUnserialized` (T10) must stay atomic across what v4 awaits. So:
  `equip_items_holding_lock(main: &Connection, mount: &Connection, …)` is
  the whole of v4's `equipUnserialized` + `assertParticipant`, SYNC, for a
  caller already inside the writer closure (P4.D328's tool handlers, this
  lane's `apply_defaults`); `equip_items(db: &Db, …)` is `assert_participant`
  (outside — v4's order), then ONE `db.write(move |w| equip_items_holding_lock(
  w.main().connection(), w.mount_index_connection(), …))`. Likewise
  `unequip_items` / `unequip_items_holding_lock`, `pick_up_items` /
  `pick_up_items_holding_lock`. `with_chat_inventory_lock(db, chat_id, f)`
  is the NAMED serialisation unit — ONE `Db::write` whose closure receives
  both writer connections; `chat_id` is for the record and the lines only.
  The pool read (main + mount) runs INSIDE the closure — the P4.D279
  `commit_equipped_outfit` precedent. `drop_carried_everywhere(main, …)` is
  SYNC and in-write (it is writes; v4's per-chat lock nests inside the one
  closure). The `Promise.all` of the four tier reads is order-free (survey
  T10) — read own / group / project / general sequentially in that order.
- **R-3 — the arrival's after-effect at `INVENTORY`.** v4's `equipDefaults`
  → `equipUnserialized` → `notifyInventoryChanged(…, {userId, provenance:

- **R-4 — `find_many_in_folder_containing` lives in `db::doc_mount_documents`

- **R-8 — the collators.** `scan_inventory_folder` (`inventory-readers.ts:

- **R-9 — the V8 twins.** `parse_inventory_item_file` and `normalize_equipped_

  ignores-order`). The `equippedInventory` CELL is `JSON.stringify` of the

## What the recorded description got wrong

The ledger's §3 rows `4fbdad186` (`drift-ledger.md:401`), `e1872c94f`
(`:402`), `102a23648` (`:403`) and §1's "Schema / D23" block are right in the
large; the keystone survey (§8) corrects them, and the ROUND BLOCK corrects
the survey's lane cut. A lane must NOT follow these parts:

- **Row `:402` — "generateDDL renders `"canManageInventory" INTEGER DEFAULT
  NULL`"** — it renders `"canManageInventory" INTEGER` (NO default: the same
  `z.boolean().nullable().optional()` shape as `"canCreateOutfits" INTEGER`
  at `fresh_schema.json:6`). `INTEGER DEFAULT NULL` is the MIGRATION's
  appended shape (`add-character-inventory-flag-v1.ts:38`). TWO shapes for
  BOTH columns (survey §3.1, D1; the round block §R.4 (b) already says so).
  The re-dump settles the fresh byte mechanically — never hand-write it.
- **Row `:401` / `:402` — the realtime `inventory` topic "under
  `e1872c94f`" and the survey's D3 ("assign the topic to lane E")** — the
  round block's Ownership gives `realtime/types.rs` (`Inventory`) to P4.D324
  (R-12). The publisher (`pickUpItems`, `equip.ts:507`) is in your
  `equip.rs`.
- **Row `:401` — "`.qtap` export spreads it, import creates it"** — no
  `lib/export` / `lib/import` hunk exists in the span (survey D5); the import's
  keyed arm (`reconcile.ts:423-440`) may route `equippedInventory` into
  `pendingOutfitNotifications` — R-S, P4.D327's measurement, NOT yours.
- **Row `:403` — "arrival defaults are ungated holds only if arrival uses
  `operator` provenance … measure at port time"** — MEASURED (survey D4,
  tools survey §8 item 3): `apply-defaults.ts:59` passes `provenance:
  'operator'` and `assertShelfReach` (`equip.ts:113-119`) returns at once for
  it. Ungated, confirmed; port as written (R-3).
- **Row `:407` — the lint "touches `pascal/roster.rs:416`"** — the `unread`
  seam is P4.D328's / P4.D332's `CustomToolLibrary` reshape (S9); P4.D340
  consumes it; nobody in this lane touches `roster.rs` (survey D6).
- **The survey's FOUR-lane cut (§5: K / E / R / L) is OVERRULED by the round
  block's SEVENTEEN-lane cut.** This lane = the survey's Lane K + Lane E
  MINUS `equip-effects.ts`'s behaviour (`describeInventoryChange`,
  `notifyInventoryChanged`, the pending map's record / strike / flush —
  P4.D328's `services/inventory_change_effects.rs`, C2), MINUS the image
  phrase (`image-description.ts`, `appearance-resolution.ts`, `avatar-prompt.
  ts`, `prompt-expansion.ts`, the two job handlers, the `refreshAvatarForAppearance`
  rename — P4.D326, §R.10 (m)), MINUS the carriers (`uuid-remap.ts`, `restore.
  ts` 22f-quater, `character-keyed-fields.ts`, the import arm, `schema-key-
  order.json`, the export schema — P4.D327, §R.10 (p)); the survey's Lane R
  is P4.D325 whole; its Lane L is P4.D340. The survey's S1 / S2 (the turn
  container + `InventoryChangeContext`) are P4.D328's C2 — with the DATA
  types declared here, in `inventory::equip` (R-1; C1 as amended 2026-10-21).
- **The survey's module paths vs C1's:** the survey proposed `db/inventory_
  location.rs` and a top-level `item_folders.rs`; C1 says `inventory::
  location` and `inventory::item_folders`. C1 wins. The survey's `inventory/
  equip_effects.rs` does not exist in this round (C2's `services/inventory_
  change_effects.rs`).
- **The survey's Lane-K line "`schema-key-order.json` + `generators/qtap-
  export.schema.json` EDIT" and headline 10's Q2 ("recommended YES" for the
  PUT key + export schema + import/restore field)** — R-6: the column and
  its create / patch / read / routing / reconcile sites are yours; the PUT
  key is P4.D328's, the import / restore field + both schema artifacts are
  P4.D327's.
- **The survey's "the orchestrator must rule whether the re-dump is taken
  WITH the `log_entries` / geometry blocks or hand-pruned" (headline 1, §3.5,
  §8 Q1)** — RULED: R-A, ONE re-dump WITH every block, taken by this lane at
  the pin, the Log and bug-255 lanes consuming it. The survey's §3.5 ALSO
  says the dumper's `count()` pass materialises `log_entries`; the vendor
  survey §E.5.3 said the dumper "will NOT see it unless the dump touches
  `getRepositories().logEntries` first" — the Log survey §3.2 settled it
  (`dump-fresh-schema.ts:113-138` calls `count()` on EVERY repository in the
  container; `LogEntriesRepository` inherits the public `count`). You STILL
  assert the seven `llmLogs` statements after the run (Tier 1 item 3).
- **Row `:402`'s "generateDDL `INTEGER DEFAULT NULL` before
  `characterDocumentMountPointId`"** — the column sits between
  `canCreateOutfits` and `canChooseOutfit` in the Zod key order
  (`character.types.ts:231-232`); `characterDocumentMountPointId` is
  elsewhere in the rendered DDL. The dump decides; the ensure appends.
- **The survey's `equip.ts` line "`notifyInventoryChanged(repos, chat,
  characterId, {equipped, unequipped: []}, ctx)`" at the end of
  `equipUnserialized`** — in v5 that call is NOT in `equip.rs` (C1: "RETURNS
  the change — never notifies"); the tuple's second element IS v4's `change`
  argument (`None` when `equipped` is empty on the equip arm; ALWAYS
  `Some({equipped: [], unequipped})` on the unequip arm — v4 calls it
  unconditionally and it early-returns on an empty change; the caller
  decides). `pick_up_items` likewise returns `Some({equipped: equip ? created
  : [], unequipped: [], picked_up: created})`.
- **`102a23648`'s `looksPlural` / `indefiniteArticle` / `withArticle` /
  `joinList` / `describeInventoryChange`** (survey §1.6 `equip-effects.ts:
  59-108`) — P4.D328's (they live in `inventory_change_effects.rs`); the
  survey's T14 article rules are its trap, not yours.
- **The survey's §2.3 transports table and §1.8** — spec for P4.D325; the
  only parts you read are the refusal → status / `{code, …details}` map
  (R-17) and the three chat actions' use of `EquipResult` / `UnequipResult`
  AS the response data (the struct field names ARE wire keys: `equipped,
  created, skipped[{id, kitId, reason}], alreadyEquipped` / `unequipped,
  notEquipped` — serde camelCase, that order).
- **The survey's §4 "runtime break" 2 (the classify-text join) and 3 / 4
  (the keyed-field list, the restore census)** — P4.D326's and P4.D327's
  reds; on THIS branch `appearance_sanitize_gate_tier3`, `chat_keyed_fields_
  equivalence` and `system_restore_state` are EXPECTED red from the pin and
  are NOT findings (§R.13). Break 1 (the arrival cell) is YOURS.

## Tiered deliverables

### Tier 1 — must land (ONE commit, `INVENTORY: …`)

The commit's CONTENT is exactly items 2–23 below (item 1 is measurement,
item 24 the follow-up header commit). Nothing a dependent compiles against or
a stacked family dumps may be left for Tier 2; nothing Tier 2 lists may be
folded in. Every item carries its family; every family is run from the pin
through the sweep driver before the commit (the per-commit gate,
`.claude/commands/commit.md` §4–§5).

1. **Red-first, before any source edit** (§R.7), counts in the record:
   - **`provisioning_equivalence` from the pin** (`QT_ORACLE_PROVISION` +

2. **The ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump at the pin (R-A).** From the pin,
   the dumper's OWN recipe (`harness/oracle/provision/dump-fresh-schema.ts:
   30-35`): `N=~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin; cd "$PIN"; QT_SCHEMA_OUT=
   /tmp/p4d324/qt-fresh-schema.json $N/npx tsx ~/source/quilltap-v5/.claude/
   worktrees/p4d324/harness/oracle/provision/dump-fresh-schema.ts` (the
   script lives in YOUR worktree; it imports v4's REAL `@/lib` from the cwd
   — run it FROM the pin). Then `cp` it over `crates/quilltap-core/src/
   services/provisioning/fresh_schema.json` — WHOLE, never a hand edit (D23).
   What the dump WILL carry beyond your two columns, each asserted after the
   run (`python3 -I -c` over the JSON, or `jq`): (a) `main`: the `chats`
   DDL gains `"equippedInventory" TEXT,` between `"equippedOutfit" TEXT,`
   and `"characterAvatars" TEXT,` (cid 66, NO default); the `characters`
   DDL gains `"canManageInventory" INTEGER,` between `"canCreateOutfits"
   INTEGER,` and `"canChooseOutfit" INTEGER DEFAULT 0,` (NO default — D1);
   the `terminal_sessions` DDL gains `"cols" REAL,\n  "rows" REAL,` between
   `"transcriptPath" TEXT,` and `"createdAt"` (bug-255 survey §3 — the
   `exitCode REAL` proof); (b) `llmLogs`: SEVEN statements in the dumper's
   name order — `CREATE TABLE "llm_logs" …`, `CREATE TABLE "log_entries"
   (\n  "id" TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,\n  "message" TEXT NOT NULL,\n
   "chatId" TEXT NOT NULL,\n  "characterId" TEXT,\n  "createdAt" TEXT NOT
   NULL,\n  "updatedAt" TEXT NOT NULL\n)`, `CREATE INDEX "idx_llm_logs_
   createdAt" …`, `CREATE INDEX "idx_llm_logs_userId" …`, `CREATE INDEX
   idx_log_entries_characterId ON log_entries ("characterId", "createdAt"
   DESC)`, `CREATE INDEX idx_log_entries_chatId ON log_entries ("chatId",
   "createdAt" DESC)`, `CREATE INDEX "idx_log_entries_createdAt" ON
   "log_entries" ("createdAt" DESC)` (Log survey §3.1–§3.2 — the two
   `onTableEnsured` indexes UNQUOTED, the generateDDL one quoted; `IF NOT
   EXISTS` dropped by `sqlite_master`); (c) `mountIndex`: UNCHANGED (diff
   it); (d) NOTHING ELSE moves — `diff <(jq … committed) <(jq … live)` lists
   exactly the four tables + the four `llmLogs` additions, else STOP and
   report (a fifth move is a v4 commit past the pin or a dirty tree — §R.2).
   `migration_indexes.json` is UNMOVED (the three migrations make no index
   — vendor survey §E.5.3); prove it by `migration_index_backfill_
   equivalence` + `host_boot_fresh_indexes` GREEN after the dump (a
   `dump-migration-indexes.ts` re-dump needs a REAL `tsx server.ts` boot —
   `QT_REAL_BOOT_MASTER` — and is NOT required; if you run one, byte-compare
   under `/tmp/p4d324/`, never into the tree). Consequences the record
   states: a FRESH v5 `setup` instance now provisions moves 13–16 together
   (`provisioning/mod.rs:500-514` replays the `llmLogs` block — no code
   change; `log_entries` and its three indexes exist from byte zero);
   `provisioning_equivalence` (1a) stays byte-exact (v5 replays the dump),
   (1b) / (1c) / (1d) GREEN on this branch with NO carve (both sides carry
   the table, the columns and the three indexes; the `BOOT_CREATED_*`
   carves untouched); `host_boot_fresh_indexes`'s promised set grows by the
   three `log_entries` names and stays green (promised = dump ∪ migration
   indexes; the first boot creates nothing extra); `fresh_ddl_for(Partition::
   LlmLogs, "log_entries")` answers the text P4.D331's `STRUCTURAL_TABLES`
   row needs (R-B); `fresh_ddl_for(Partition::Main, "terminal_sessions")`
   carries the REAL geometry P4.D337's ensure family compares against. On an
   EXISTING instance the two Inventory columns arrive by item 3's ensures,
   the geometry by P4.D337's, `log_entries` by R-B — nothing here.
3. **`crates/quilltap-core/src/db/inventory_columns_repair.rs` — the two
   ensures (the THIRTEENTH + FOURTEENTH moves on an existing instance) +
   their wiring + the NEW `inventory_columns_ensure_equivalence` family.**
   Module header carrying v4's why (the two migration headers; "the Inventory
   objects each character carries" / "gates the inventory_* tools") and the
   two-shape record (survey §3.1: `TEXT` / `INTEGER` rendered on a fresh
   instance — the dump's bytes; `TEXT DEFAULT NULL` / `INTEGER DEFAULT NULL`
   appended by the migration on an existing one — BOTH legal, a `DEFAULT
   NULL` is semantically no default; the P4.D249 `chat_informs.permanent`
   class, NOT the P4.D304 byte-equal one — T7). `MIGRATION_ID_EQUIPPED_
   INVENTORY = "add-chat-equipped-inventory-v1"`, `MIGRATION_ID_INVENTORY_
   FLAG = "add-character-inventory-flag-v1"`, `EQUIPPED_INVENTORY_DECL =
   "TEXT DEFAULT NULL"`, `INVENTORY_FLAG_DECL = "INTEGER DEFAULT NULL"`;
   `ensure_chats_equipped_inventory(main) -> Result<bool, DbError>` =
   `table_exists("chats")` gate + `schema_probe::add_column_if_missing(main,
   "chats", "equippedInventory", EQUIPPED_INVENTORY_DECL)` (its text IS v4's
   `ALTER TABLE "chats" ADD COLUMN "equippedInventory" TEXT DEFAULT NULL` —
   confirm `add_column_if_missing`'s quoting against v4 `database-utils.ts`'s
   `addColumnIfMissing` at the pin, as P4.D304 did); `ensure_characters_can_
   manage_inventory(main)` the same over `characters`; `run_inventory_columns_
   ensures(main) -> Result<(), DbError>` runs BOTH in v4's registration order
   (chats, then characters — `index.ts:924-926`), logging v5's ONE INFO per
   added column (R-5: `target: "quilltap::boot"`, `migrationId`, `columnAdded
   = true`), the first `Err` returning (FATAL — neither migration is
   `resumable`). UNSTAMPED (survey §3.2: v4 records nothing for a `shouldRun`
   false, so after v5's ALTER v4's runner never re-runs — no ping-pong;
   nothing later drops the columns). Three unit tests in the module (the
   `chat_settings_4_11_repair.rs` trio): a pre-column `chats` + `characters`
   → both appended in order with the exact `sqlite_master` tails (`,
   "equippedInventory" TEXT DEFAULT NULL)` / `, "canManageInventory" INTEGER

   `pub mod inventory_columns_repair;` in the repair region;
   `restore_vintage_state.rs` after `:187` — the boot-align block (the

   [&str; 2]` + `run_chain` calling `run_inventory_columns_ensures` LAST +
   the `v4 re-runs NOTHING after v5's chain` assertion on `planted` / `fresh`
   / `fresh-used` / `v4-migrated` for the two ids (UNSTAMPED, the P4.D304 arm
   at `:276` — the `-unstamped` twins re-run exactly what they did before);
   `harness/oracle/cases/zero-rerun.ts:148-152` spreads `INVENTORY_
   MIGRATIONS` after `CHAT_WARDROBE_HISTORY_MIGRATIONS`. **The family:**
   `crates/quilltap-harness/tests/inventory_columns_ensure_equivalence.rs`
   over `harness/oracle/cases/inventory-columns-ensure.ts` (tsx — the
   `chat-settings-4-11-columns-ensure.ts` shape, header copied): SEVEN base

    sections: opener_inventory.into_iter().collect()` on BOTH `GreetingRequest`

(`--families` takes the FULL test-file stem, comma-separated) — ONE sweep at
a time machine-wide, announced to the human first; a family you do not OWN
(the wardrobe net, the capstone / cast / participants / merge families, the
column-plumbing families) is re-run by a hand copy of its recipe with every
output under `/tmp/p4d324/` (memory note `parallel-lane-shared-resources`),
and its NDJSON `cmp`'d against the previous regen where "unchanged" is the
claim. `rm -f` each NDJSON before its regen; read the builder's last line;

passes). A family reporting `SKIP` in 0.00 s did not run; put every
`QT_ORACLE_*` inline on every call (memory note `oracle-runner-and-skip-
traps`). Recipe headers canonical (`cd ~/source/quilltap-server`, `V5W=
${V5W:-$HOME/source/quilltap-v5}`, `N=~/.nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin`,
the jest ones with `--testTimeout=180000` and `--runTestsByPath` where a path
carries `[id]` — memory note `v4-path-quoting-traps`; jest cases run from a
`/tmp` mirror because jest ignores `.claude/` paths — copy `lib/v4-
migrations.ts` into the mirror's `lib/`); `recipe_sweep.py --self-test` exits
0 after the new headers. Every jest oracle writes ZERO `llm_logs` rows except

oracle-gotchas`); every seed id is a UUID (memory note `memory-raw-reads-drop-
non-uuid-fixtures`).

## 💸 for the dogfood pass

No LLM spend in this lane's own slice (the image generation is canned both
sides; a REAL object picture is P4.D326's / the SPA's row). On a Friday copy
taken at ANY v4 point (the standing 2026-10-19 note; ⚠ ONLY with the union
build or a PRE-round build — §S.6's LIVE-DATA restatement):

- **Record the copy's vintage FIRST** (before any v5 boot): `PRAGMA
  table_info(chats)` (is `equippedInventory` present, and at cid 66 or
  LAST?), `PRAGMA table_info(characters)` (`canManageInventory`), `SELECT
  id FROM migrations_state WHERE id IN ('add-chat-equipped-inventory-v1',
  'add-character-inventory-flag-v1')`, `SELECT count(*) FROM chats WHERE
  "equippedInventory" IS NOT NULL` and the distinct shapes of that cell
  (`{"<id>":[]}` stamps vs carried sets), `sqlite_master` for `log_entries`
  in the llm-logs file → mode A (pre-`4fbdad186`: both columns absent), mode
  B (v4 migrated: both appended LAST with `DEFAULT NULL`, two ledger rows),
  or a fresh-shape instance.
- **The first v5 boot** on that copy: on mode A the two INFOs in order and
  the columns appended with the migrations' clauses, nothing stamped, a
  second boot silent; on mode B every ensure a NO-OP (no INFO, no new ledger
  row); `/health` sound; `log_entries` CREATED by the structural pass on the
  llm-logs file (P4.D331's row — observe, record).
- **A chat created in v5 with two characters** → the `chats` row's
  `equippedInventory` = `{"<a>":[],"<b>":[]}` in participant order; a seat
  added later gains its key; a continuation from a chat whose character
  carries objects (v4-written) copies the set VERBATIM; a re-seat of a
  removed character is NOT revisited (the `[]` stamp).
- **A real Salon turn** by a character whose v4-written carried set names a
  described object → `## Carried and equipped` in the LLM Inspector AFTER
  the progressions section and BEFORE an inform block (plant a standing
  inform to see the order); the same on a Continue (bug 253); a Carina
  question (Tier 2); a NEW chat's greeting (the opener — Tier 2) when the
  character carries an `equippedByDefault` object.
- **The `[InventoryArrival]` lines** in the server log on each of the above;
  `Equipped inventory write` with `written: true`; on a copy whose `chats`
  lacks the column (open the data dir with a v5 build WITHOUT the host —
  the CLI's read path) nothing writes and nothing errors.
- **A Scriptorium write** of a hand-made `Inventory/<name>.json` (an object
  with a UUID `id`) into Friday's vault → the next pool read lists it (through
  P4.D325's `GET …/inventory` once landed; until then `quilltap db` on the
  copy: `SELECT relativePath FROM doc_mount_file_links WHERE relativePath LIKE
  'Inventory/%'`), a file with a duplicate id left alone with the WARN.
- The equip / unequip / pick-up / give gestures, the cap refusal's `details`
  in the SPA toast, the title lint, the object picture (💸) and the backup /
  restore round trip of `equippedInventory` + `canManageInventory` are the
  round's §S.8 rows owned by P4.D325 / P4.D328 / P4.D330 / P4.D340 / P4.D326
  / P4.D327 — list them in the record as consumers of this lane's work, not
  as this lane's rows.

    structural_tables` + the provisioning replay of the `llmLogs` block.
    Hence R-B: ONE `STRUCTURAL_TABLES` row + the re-dump; no ensure, no
    stamp, nothing for `zero_migrations_rerun` / `restore_vintage_state`.

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Tag ownership (each order's survey §1 lists the bytes):** P4.D324 the two ensures' v5-worded INFOs (**migration lines follow the standing precedent** — P4.D249 / P4.D304 / P4.D315, `host.rs:2176-2181`: v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column; P4.D324 R-5, P4.D337 R-1), `Failed to write equipped inventory` + `Equipped inventory
    write`, the pool / folder / mutation lines, the arrival-default lines;
    P4.D325 the route lines; P4.D326 the appearance / image lines + the
    bug-133 re-ruling's changed lines; P4.D327 the restore `22f-quater`
    lines + the three new `RESTORE_TS_MESSAGES` entries (the census moves
    in BOTH lists); P4.D328 the nine handlers' lines, the flush / notify
    lines, `Custom tool carries keys this build does not know`'s siblings
    only where the tools reshape owns them; P4.D329 the roster's inventory
    DEBUGs, the bench's lines, the applier's inventory lines; P4.D331
    `Log entries pruned`, `Error cleaning up old log entries`, `Custom tool
    log effect failed; skipped`, the grep / read DEBUGs, the regexp compile
    errors as `UserFunctionError` text; P4.D332 `Custom tool definition
    failed schema validation` (REPLACING the loader's `Custom tool rejected
    at load time`), the `edit_custom_tool` lines; P4.D333 `Custom tool table
    dealt ahead of the consult` + the any-of DEBUGs; P4.D335 the
    `[Interrupt]` lines; P4.D337 the geometry ensure's v5-worded INFO + the Ariel writer's two DEBUG lines + `Terminal read executed` + the screen / read lines; P4.D338 the sort / photos / uri lines; P4.D340 the
    lint lines. A lane whose capture sees a sibling's line in the same
    request asserts its PRESENCE by tag and leaves its bytes to the owner.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    stage-A lanes, on the `INVENTORY` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. **This round plans these:** `SCREEN_RESIZE_NO_
    REFLOW` (P4.D337, R-D); `REGEXP_ENGINE_GAP` (P4.D331 — only if R-I's
    engine cannot compile a V8 pattern identically; one row per listed
    syntax); `CHILD_BUFFER_VISIBILITY` (P4.D331 — v5's in-process writer
    makes a same-turn `log_read` see the line v4's job child hides);
    `LONE_SURROGATE_CLIP` (P4.D331, only if measured); `PHOTOS_VAULT_
    EXCLUSION` is NOT a divergence (v5 converges to v4). Recorded-only
    classes (no table, by ruling): the SPA's query-key spellings; the
    `withArticle` client rule (`an unicorn horn`) vs the server's line
    (P4.D330 ports the client rule verbatim and records the pair); v5's
    sorted drain order.
  - **BY-DESIGN flips (ledger §5.4 — measure, then retire / re-aim, never
    delete unread):** `provisioning_equivalence` (1d; 1b / 1c for the
    `log_entries` indexes) and `migration_index_backfill` at the pin
    (P4.D324); `table_shape_equivalence` (P4.D331); the appearance-sanitize
    grid's INPUT shape (P4.D326); the `uri-self` / `res-self` rows and the
    Librarian's `ds-self` ×3 (P4.D338); `tool_definitions` (+13) /
    `tools_inventory` (58 rows, `pascal`) / `pascal_tool_vocabulary` (the
    five new keys) / `pascal_run_custom` (the preamble sentence) — union
    reds each lane carves by name and the unifier lifts (R-H); the
    definition corpus's inventory / log / any-of / brief rows (R-F); the
    `full-precedence-chain` and `characterIds` rows (P4.D332); the help /
    docs / schema guards (§R.3). No port filing returns in the span (bugs
    252–257 are v4-originated; the ledger's convergence check is NEGATIVE;
    `406dbdd5a` is a RE-RULING, not a convergence — its grid is re-shaped,
    not retired).
- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory note
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4347 and mocks 45200 / 45301–45304 / 45340–45345 are taken):
    **P4.D330 4348 / mock 45346** (`e2e/inventory-flow.spec.ts`), **P4.D334
    4349 / mock 45347** (`e2e/workbench-grammar-flow.spec.ts`), **P4.D336
    4350 / mock 45348** (`e2e/salon-interrupt-flow.spec.ts`), **P4.D337
    4351 / mock 45349** (if a terminal e2e is added), **P4.D338 4352 /
    mock 45350** (if a photos e2e is added). A lane that only edits an
    EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-20 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone); a stacked lane may clone its parent lane's
    worktree target instead, when that lane is not mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test.
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    seventeen lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves).

- **§R.11 — Rulings for the human.** Each order's "Rulings made at
  planning" are the planner's calls; the human may overrule any before lane
  start (an overruled ruling is a STOP item for that lane). The round-level
  ones to read FIRST (none is yet approved — the human approves or
  overrules at the orders' hand-off; **R-C and R-I add a NEW crate each — both APPROVED by the human on 2026-10-21**):
  - **THE PIN (human, 2026-10-09, standing)** — §R.1 / §R.2: `322a5488e`,
    v4 HEAD at planning.
  - **R-A — ONE `fresh_schema.json` re-dump at the pin, owned by the
    keystone, carrying ALL FOUR moves (13–16).** The dumper's own `count()`
    pass materialises `log_entries` and the geometry columns, so the dump
    carries them whether or not the Log / bug-255 lanes have landed; D23
    forbids a hand-pruned dump. P4.D324 commits it at `INVENTORY`; P4.D331
    and P4.D337 read it (`fresh_ddl_for(Partition::LlmLogs, "log_entries")`;
    the terminal pair). Consequence: on a FRESH v5 instance the four moves
    provision together the moment `INVENTORY` lands; on an EXISTING instance
    the two Inventory columns arrive by P4.D324's ensures, the geometry
    columns by P4.D337's ensure, and `log_entries` by R-B.
  - **R-B — `log_entries` provisions through the structural pass**: a
    `fresh_schema.json` row (generateDDL renders it, so D23's re-dump rule
    applies) + ONE `STRUCTURAL_TABLES` row so `create_missing_structural_
    tables` makes it on an existing instance exactly as v4's PHASE 3.1 does
    (checked 11 → 12). NO dedicated ensure, NO `migrations_state` stamp,
    nothing in `zero_migrations_rerun` / `restore_vintage_state`. This is
    consistent with the 2026-10-19 "boot ensures, never v4-dumped artifacts"
    ruling, whose subject is MIGRATION-ONLY tables; `log_entries` has no
    migration and v4 itself creates it from the schema. The human confirms
    this reading (Log survey §8 Q1).
  - **R-C — bug 255's terminal emulator is `vt100` 0.18.1** (MIT, MSRV 1.85,
    deps `vte` 0.15 + `unicode-width` + `itoa`; `cargo info vt100` reaches
    the registry): it reproduces every behaviour v4's `screen.ts` relies on
    (`?1049` with save / restore + clear, absolute / relative cursor moves,
    `CUF`, `EL` / `ED`, per-row wrap flags, capped scrollback, `on_scroll`)
    except reflow on resize. `alacritty_terminal` drags a PTY / event loop;
    `termwiz` is not a screen model; `wezterm-term` is not on crates.io;
    `vte` is parser-only. **A NEW dependency — APPROVED by the human on 2026-10-21 ("Approved: vt100 0.18.1 for P4.D337")**; P4.D337 may start once `INVENTORY` lands.
  - **R-D — reflow on resize is a RECORDED divergence** (`SCREEN_RESIZE_NO_
    REFLOW`, both-ways rows), not re-implemented: live sessions resize
    rarely and `renderTranscript` never resizes. The human may instead
    require a re-wrap of the normal buffer on `cols` change (~200 lines
    over vt100's rows).
  - **R-E — the custom-tool schema + the two specimens are ENGINE inputs
    now.** P4.D332 embeds crate-local copies (`include_str!`) of the pin's
    `public/schemas/qtap-custom-tool.schema.json` (63,595 B),
    `docs/developer/CUSTOM_TOOL_SPEC.json` and `CUSTOM_TOOL_SPEC_DICE.json`
    under a NEW `custom_tool_schema_embed_guard` comparing bytes against
    P4.D333's vendored SPA copy and P4.D339's `docs/v4/` mirrors; `docs/v4/`
    stays a pure mirror (never a build input). The pin's `CUSTOM_TOOL_SPEC.
    json` exercises `inventory`, `inventoryCount`, an any-of `when`,
    `inventoryAggregate` and a `log` effect — v4's "every specimen passes
    the real validator" test is a UNION obligation (§S.5 wire 9): green only
    once P4.D329 + P4.D331 + P4.D333 land; P4.D332 records it red on its
    branch by row.
  - **R-F — the shared definition corpus** (`pascal-custom-tool-definition`,
    458 → N rows) is regenerated from the pin ONCE by P4.D333 for both
    consumers (the server family and the SPA copy); P4.D329 and P4.D331
    merge their rows BY ID and each proves ITS rows green on its branch,
    naming the sibling-caused residue; the unifier re-runs the one regen on
    the union and the family must be green whole. P4.D333's SPA twin parses
    EVERY grammar (inventory, aggregate, any-of, brief, log) so the SPA copy
    is green on its own branch.
  - **R-G — union strings:** every lane that writes one of the FOUR
    effect-kind sentences, the three "must test …" sentences, the
    `unrecognized_keys` list or the `resolveEffects` skip text writes the
    PIN's whole bytes (§R.4 (d)); the merge is then textual. The
    struct-literal rule (§R.10 (d)): P4.D328 adds `pending_inventory_
    announcements` LAST; nobody renames, reorders or reflows.
  - **R-H — the GENERATED catalog:** each lane regenerates `tools/
    definitions/data.rs` from the pin and carves the siblings' entries by
    name in `tool_definitions_equivalence` ("no extras" lifted per sibling
    set) and `tools_inventory_equivalence`; the unifier regenerates ONCE on
    the union from the pin's `ALL_TOOLS` order, sets `definitions/mod.rs`'s
    assert to 76 and `tools_inventory`'s to 58, and lifts every carve.
  - **R-I — the `regexp()` scalar's engine is `regress`** (ECMAScript regex
    in Rust; NOT in `Cargo.lock` — a NEW crate). The `regex` crate lacks
    lookaround / backreferences and differs on `\d` / `\w` / `.` / `i` / `m`
    semantics and on every error text; `fancy-regex` is transitive but not
    ECMAScript. **A NEW dependency — APPROVED by the human on 2026-10-21 ("… and regress for P4.D331's regexp() unit")**; the lane MEASURES the current `regress` version at lane start and pins it exactly. Every OTHER Log unit (the table, the
    repository, `log_create_entry`, `log_read`, the cleanup prune, the
    Pascal `log` effect, the Brahma / `run_sql` prompt bytes) lands first.
    If the human refuses, `log_grep` lands over `regress`-less typed
    refusal rows (`REGEXP_ENGINE_GAP`, one per listed syntax) and the
    V8 error catalogue (Log survey §6 T3) is pinned where the chosen engine
    can match it. The error text is `Invalid regular expression:
    /<pattern>/<flags>: <V8 message>` (post-`a495faa26`), surfaced as
    `rusqlite::Error::UserFunctionError`.
  - **R-J — the interrupts need NO new dependency**: the stop is
    cooperative — an `AtomicBool` + `tokio::sync::Notify` raced in
    `WatchedStream::recv` through `futures_util::future::select` (the core
    build has `tokio` `sync` + `time` only, no `macros`, no `tokio-util`;
    the `activity_registry.rs` precedent hand-rolled its task-local for the
    same reason). The registry is a CORE `static` keyed by chat id (v4's
    `processSingleton` twin — reachable from the engine's action arms AND
    the host's spine thread; tests use unique chat ids), with the handle
    threaded EXPLICITLY through `OrchestratorDeps` → `FinalizeOptions` / the
    loop opts / the recovery opts / the watchdog context, `None` for the
    enclave and the canned differentials. The autonomous enclave turn is
    NOT registered (v4: the job child; recorded as a deliberate
    non-capability). `ChatSendResultDto` gains nothing (YAGNI); the two
    REST arms are Tier 2 (the `inform` arm is the precedent).
  - **R-K — the keystone carries `setEquippedInventory`'s caught-failure
    lines** (`Failed to write equipped inventory` + DEBUG `Equipped
    inventory write`) as captured pins — a v5 opened on a pre-column v4 DB
    (the migration runner is deferred) hits exactly that arm.
  - **R-L — `CoreError` / `CoreDispatchError` grows `details`** (option (a)
    of the SPA survey §8.1): ONE lane (P4.D325) carries the Rust envelope's EXISTING
    `CoreError.details` (measured present at `5a94a9d1e`) through every
    transport's error path (the axum writer, Tauri, the SSE error frame —
    measure which already pass it) and adds `details?: Record<string,
    unknown>` to the SPA's `CoreDispatchError` (P4.D330's fence) so the Inventory's
    four coded refusals (`CAP_EXCEEDED` → `itemName, cap`; `NOT_COPYABLE` →
    `itemIds`; `ARCHETYPE_IN_USE` → `dependents, holders[].ownerName`;
    `ARCHIVED_DEPENDENTS` → `characters[].name`) reach the client as v4's
    `{code, ...details}` bytes; P4.D330 reads `error.details ?? {}`. The
    wardrobe's `generate` REST precedent is NOT followed for the Inventory
    writes (they ride dispatch). Every other lane leaves `details` absent.
  - **R-M — ONE `inventory*` verb family with a `container: { scope, id }`

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** `INVENTORY` (P4.D324) is ONE commit whose
  subject starts with its name; the owner records the sha in its status
  header ON ITS LANE BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane (P4.D325,
  P4.D326, P4.D327, P4.D328, P4.D329, P4.D331, P4.D337, P4.D340) finds the
  sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all --format='%h %s' | grep -E
  '^[0-9a-f]+ INVENTORY:'`, confirmed against the parent's lane-branch copy
  of the order) and branches from it — never from `main`, never a guessed
  sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and report. A stacked lane
  may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none of C1's names (P4.D331's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + `regexp_function.rs` + the three tool definitions;
  P4.D337's `terminal_screen.rs` + its tier-1 family; P4.D326's bug-133
  re-ruling; P4.D327's `character_keyed_fields.rs` five-field twin + the
  export schema; P4.D333-independent parts of P4.D329 such as `inventory_
  aggregate.rs`'s pure fold; P4.D335 has no parent), rebasing onto
  `INVENTORY` before the first commit that does — that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". P4.D330, P4.D332, P4.D333,
  P4.D334, P4.D335, P4.D336, P4.D338 and P4.D339 cut from `main` at the
  orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D324 (→ `INVENTORY`,
    the Opus / Fable tier — its Tier 1 is large but bounded), and in
    parallel P4.D333 (the grammar twins — the second keystone-class lane),
    P4.D335 (interrupts server), P4.D332, P4.D338, P4.D339 (cheap, Sonnet
    tier). That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** P4.D330,
    P4.D334, P4.D336 (the three SPA lanes — each authors against the
    contracts with gated beats; Sonnet tier with Opus for P4.D330).
  - **Wave A, from `INVENTORY`:** P4.D328 and P4.D329 FIRST (the two Pascal /
    tools consumers every later union depends on), then P4.D325, P4.D326,
    P4.D327, P4.D331 (`regress` approved), P4.D340 (last — it consumes P4.D328's library reshape + `resolve_inventory_ref`, P4.D329's gate-group records and P4.D333's `gate_groups` by contract, each a fenced fail-closed stand-in on its own branch),
    P4.D337 (`vt100` approved).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
